Reducido

Tie-breaks.
 Qualification stage
  Head-to-head points.
  Head-to-head goal difference.
  Head-to-head goals for.
  Head-to-head goals away.
  Repeat the previous steps in case of partial resolution.
  Goal difference in the group.
  Goals for in the group.
  Goals away in the group.
  Lot.
 Knock-out and Final stages.
  Goal difference in the two matches.
  PSO.
